data: {"agent":"specialist-toa","file":"src/utils/audio.ts","content":"import { WaveformType } from '../types';\n\ninterface ActiveVoice {\n  oscillator: OscillatorNode;\n  gainNode: GainNode;\n ActiveVoice> = new Map();\n  private currentWaveform: WaveformType = 'triangle';\n  private currentVolume: number = 0.5;\n\n  private initContextIfNeeded(): AudioContext {\n    if (!this.audioCtx) {\n      const AudioCtxClass = window.AudioContext || (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;\n      this.audioCtx = new AudioCtxClass();\n      \n      this.masterGain = this.audioCtx.createGain();\n      this.masterGain.gain.setValueAtTime(this.currentVolume, this.audioCtx.currentTime);\n      this.masterGain.connect(this.audioCtx.destination);\n    }\n\n    if (this.audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {\n      this.audioCtx.resume();\n    }\n\n    return this.audioCtx;\n  }\n\n  public setWaveform(type: WaveformType): void {\n    this.currentWaveform = type;\n  }\n\n  public getWaveform(): WaveformType {\n    return this.currentWaveform;\n  }\n\n  public setVolume(volume: number): void {\n    this.currentVolume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume));\n    if (this.masterGain && this.audioCtx) {\n      const now = this.audioCtx.currentTime;\n      this.masterGain.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n      this.masterGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(this.currentVolume, now, 0.02);\n    }\n  }\n\n  public getVolume(): number {\n    return this.currentVolume;\n  }\n\n  public startNote(noteId: string, frequency: number): void {\n    const ctx = this.initContextIfNeeded();\n    if (!this.masterGain) return;\n\n    if (this.activeVoices.has(noteId)) {\n      this.stopNote(noteId);\n    }\n\n    const now = ctx.currentTime;\n    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();\n    const voiceGain = ctx.createGain();\n    const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();\n\n    osc.type = this.currentWaveform;\n    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, now);\n\n    filter.type = 'lowpass';\n    filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.min(frequency * 5, 12000), now);\n    filter.Q.setValueAtTime(1, now);\n\n    // ADSR Envelope\n    const attackTime = 0.015;\n    const decayTime = 0.12;\n    const sustainLevel = 0.65;\n\n    voiceGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, now);\n    voiceGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1.0, now + attackTime);\n    voiceGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(sustainLevel, now + attackTime + decayTime);\n\n    osc.connect(filter);\n    filter.connect(voiceGain);\n    voiceGain.connect(this.masterGain);\n\n    osc.start(now);\n\n    this.activeVoices.set(noteId, {\n      oscillator: osc,\n      gainNode: voiceGain,\n      filterNode: filter\n    });\n  }\n\n  public stopNote(noteId: string): void {\n    const voice = this.activeVoices.get(noteId);\n    if (!voice || !this.audioCtx) return;\n\n    const ctx = this.audioCtx;\n    const now = ctx.currentTime;\n    const releaseTime = 0.18;\n\n    voice.gainNode.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n    const currentGain = Math.max(voice.gainNode.gain.value, 0.0001);\n    voice.gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(currentGain, now);\n    voice.gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now + releaseTime);\n\n    voice.oscillator.stop(now + releaseTime + 0.02);\n\n event: file-complete
data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/PianoKeyboard.tsx","content":"import { PianoKeyItem } from '../types';\n\ninterface PianoKeyboardProps {\n  keys?: PianoKeyItem[];\n  activeNotes?: Set<string>;\n  showNoteNames?: boolean;\n  showKeyLabels?: boolean;\n  onNoteStart?: (noteId: string) => void;\n  onNoteStop?: (noteId: string) => void;\n}\n\nconst PianoKeyboard = ({\n  keys = [],\n  activeNotes = new Set<string>(),\n  showNoteNames = true,\n  showKeyLabels = true,\n  onNoteStart,\n  onNoteStop\n}: PianoKeyboardProps) => {\n  const whiteKeys = keys.filter((k) => !k.isBlack);\n  const blackKeys = keys.filter((k) => k.isBlack);\n\n  const handlePointerDown = (noteId: string, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    (e.target as HTMLElement).setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);\n    onNoteStart?.(noteId);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = (noteId: string, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    if ((e.target as HTMLElement).hasPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId)) {\n      (e.target as HTMLElement).releasePointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);\n    }\n    onNoteStop?.(noteId);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerEnter = (noteId: string, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    if (e.buttons === 1) {\n      onNoteStart?.(noteId);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = (noteId: string, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    if (e.buttons === 1) {\n      onNoteStop?.(noteId);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"keyboard-outer\">\n      <div className=\"keyboard-scroll\">\n        <div className=\"piano-keyboard\">\n          <div className=\"white-keys-container\">\n            {whiteKeys.map((keyItem) => {\n              const isActive = activeNotes.has(keyItem.id);\n              const keyClassName = isActive ? 'key white-key key-active' : 'key white-key';\n\n              return (\n                <div\n                  key={keyItem.id}\n                  className={keyClassName}\n                  onPointerDown={(e) => handlePointerDown(keyItem.id, e)}\n                  onPointerUp={(e) => handlePointerUp(keyItem.id, e)}\n                  onPointerEnter={(e) => handlePointerEnter(keyItem.id, e)}\n                  onPointerLeave={(e) => handlePointerLeave(keyItem.id, e)}\n                  onPointerCancel={(e) => handlePointerUp(keyItem.id, e)}\n                >\n                  <div className=\"key-label-wrapper\">\n                    {showNoteNames && (\n                      <span className=\"key-label-note\">{keyItem.noteLabel}</span>\n                    )}\n                    {showKeyLabels && (\n                      <span className=\"key-label-key\">{keyItem.keyboardKey.toUpperCase()}</span>\n                    )}\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              );\n            })}\n          </div>\n\n          <div className=\"black-keys-container\">\n            {blackKeys.map((keyItem) => {\n              const isActive = activeNotes.has(keyItem.id);\n              const keyClassName = isActive ? 'key black-key key-active' : 'key black-key';\n              const leftPos = keyItem.leftPercent !== undefined ? `${keyItem.leftPercent}%` : '0%';\n\n              return (\n                <div\n                  key={keyItem.id}\n                  className={keyClassName}\n                  style={{ left: leftPos }}\n                  onPointerDown={(e) => handlePointerDown(keyItem.id, e)}\n                  onPointerUp={(e) => handlePointerUp(keyItem.id, e)}\n                  onPointerEnter={(e) => handlePointerEnter(keyItem.id, e)}\n                  onPointerLeave={(e) => handlePointerLeave(keyItem.id, e)}\n                  onPointerCancel={(e) => handlePointerUp(keyItem.id, e)}\n                >\n                  <div className=\"key-label-wrapper\">\n                    {showNoteNames && (\n                      <span className=\"key-label-note\">{keyItem.noteLabel}</span>\n                    )}\n                    {showKeyLabels && (\n                      <span className=\"key-label-key\">{keyItem.keyboardKey.toUpperCase()}</span>\n                    )}\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              );\n            })}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PianoKeyboard;","language":"tsx"}

filterNode: BiquadFilterNode;\n}\n\nconst MAX_POLYPHONY = 12;\n\nclass AudioEngine {\n  private audioCtx: AudioContext | null = null;\n  private masterGain: GainNode | null = null;\n  private activeVoices: Map<string, ActiveVoice> = new Map();\n  private currentWaveform: WaveformType = 'triangle';\n  private currentVolume: number = 0.5;\n\n  private initContextIfNeeded(): AudioContext {\n    if (!this.audioCtx) {\n      const AudioCtxClass = window.AudioContext || (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;\n      this.audioCtx = new AudioCtxClass();\n      \n      this.masterGain = this.audioCtx.createGain();\n      this.masterGain.gain.setValueAtTime(this.currentVolume, this.audioCtx.currentTime);\n      this.masterGain.connect(this.audioCtx.destination);\n    }\n\n    if (this.audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {\n      this.audioCtx.resume().catch((err) => {\n        console.warn('AudioContext resume was prevented or failed:', err);\n      });\n    }\n\n    return this.audioCtx;\n  }\n\n  public setWaveform(type: WaveformType): void {\n    this.currentWaveform = type;\n  }\n\n  public getWaveform(): WaveformType {\n    return this.currentWaveform;\n  }\n\n  public setVolume(volume: number): void {\n    this.currentVolume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume));\n    if (this.masterGain && this.audioCtx) {\n      const now = this.audioCtx.currentTime;\n      this.masterGain.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n      this.masterGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(this.currentVolume, now, 0.02);\n    }\n  }\n\n  public getVolume(): number {\n    return this.currentVolume;\n  }\n\n  public startNote(noteId: string, frequency: number): void {\n    const ctx = this.initContextIfNeeded();\n    if (!this.masterGain) return;\n\n    if (this.activeVoices.has(noteId)) {\n      this.stopNote(noteId);\n    }\n\n    // ボイススティーリング (最大同時発音数の制限超過時に最古のボイスを解放)\n    if (this.activeVoices.size >= MAX_POLYPHONY) {\n      const oldestNoteId = this.activeVoices.keys().next().value;\n      if (oldestNoteId) {\n        this.stopNote(oldestNoteId);\n      }\n    }\n\n    const now = ctx.currentTime;\n    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();\n    const voiceGain = ctx.createGain();\n    const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();\n\n    osc.type = this.currentWaveform;\n    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, now);\n\n    filter.type = 'lowpass';\n    filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.min(frequency * 5, 12000), now);\n    filter.Q.setValueAtTime(1, now);\n\n    // ADSR Envelope\n    const attackTime = 0.015;\n    const decayTime = 0.12;\n    const sustainLevel = 0.65;\n\n    voiceGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, now);\n    voiceGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1.0, now + attackTime);\n    voiceGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(sustainLevel, now + attackTime + decayTime);\n\n    osc.connect(filter);\n    filter.connect(voiceGain);\n    voiceGain.connect(this.masterGain);\n\n    // 再生完了時にノードの切断を安全に実施 (タイマー遅延・リーク防止)\n    osc.onended = () => {\n      osc.disconnect();\n      filter.disconnect();\n      voiceGain.disconnect();\n    };\n\n    osc.start(now);\n\n    this.activeVoices.set(noteId, {\n      oscillator: osc,\n      gainNode: voiceGain,\n      filterNode: filter\n    });\n  }\n\n  public stopNote(noteId: string): void {\n    const voice = this.activeVoices.get(noteId);\n    if (!voice || !this.audioCtx) return;\n\n    const ctx = this.audioCtx;\n    const now = ctx.currentTime;\n    const releaseTime = 0.18;\n\n    voice.gainNode.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n    const currentGain = Math.max(voice.gainNode.gain.value, 0.0001);\n    voice.gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(currentGain, now);\n    voice.gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now + releaseTime);\n\n    // stop() 呼び出し後に onended コールバックで disconnect() が実行される\n    voice.oscillator.stop(now + releaseTime + 0.02);\n\n    this.activeVoices.delete(noteId);\n  }\n\n  public stopAllNotes(): void {\n    const noteIds = Array.from(this.activeVoices.keys());\n    for PianoKeyboard = ({\n  keys = [],\n  activeNotes = new Set<string>(),\n  showNoteNames = true,\n  showKeyLabels = true,\n  onNoteStart,\n  onNoteStop\n}: PianoKeyboardProps) => {\n  const whiteKeys = keys.filter((k) => !k.isBlack);\n  const blackKeys = keys.filter((k) => k.isBlack);\n\n  const handlePointerDown = (noteId: string, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    onNoteStart?.(noteId);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = (noteId: string, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    onNoteStop?.(noteId);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerEnter = (noteId: string, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    if (e.buttons === 1) {\n      onNoteStart?.(noteId);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = (noteId: string, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    if (e.buttons === 1) {\n      onNoteStop?.(noteId);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleKeyDown = (noteId: string, e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {\n    if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      if (!activeNotes.has(noteId)) {\n        onNoteStart?.(noteId);\n      }\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleKeyUp = (noteId: string, e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {\n    if (e.key      let intervalId = null;\n        self.onmessage = function(e) {\n          if (e.data === 'start') {\n            if (intervalId) clearInterval(intervalId);\n            intervalId = setInterval(function() {\n              self.postMessage('tick');\n            }, 15);\n          } else if (e.data === 'stop') {\n            if (intervalId) {\n              clearInterval(intervalId);\n              intervalId = null;\n            }\n          }\n        };\n      `\n      ],\n      { type: 'application/javascript' }\n    );\n\n    const workerUrl = URL.createObjectURL(workerBlob);\n    const worker = new Worker(workerUrl);\n\n    worker.onmessage = () => {\n      if (!sequenceTypeRef.current) return;\n\n      const elapsed = performance.now() - sequenceStartTimeRef.current;\n      const events = sequenceEventsRef.current;\n\n      while (\n        sequenceEventIndexRef.current < events.length &&\n        events[sequenceEventIndexRef.current].time <= elapsed\n      ) {\n        const ev = events[sequenceEventIndexRef.current];\n        if (ev.type === 'start') {\n          playNote(ev.noteId);\n        } else {\n          stopNote(ev.noteId);\n        }\n        sequenceEventIndexRef.current += 1;\n      }\n\n      if (\n        sequenceEventIndexRef.current >= events.length &&\n        elapsed >= sequenceTotalDurationRef.current\n      ) {\n        const type = sequenceTypeRef.current;\n        stopSequencer();\n        if (type === 'recorded') {\n          setIsPlayingRecorded(false);\n        } else if (type === 'demo') {\n          setIsPlayingDemo(false);\n        }\n      }\n    };\n\n    workerRef.current = worker;\n\n    return () => {\n      worker.postMessage('stop');\n      worker.terminate();\n      URL.revokeObjectURL(workerUrl);\n    };\n  }, [playNote, stopNote, stopSequencer]);\n\n  const handleWaveformChange = (newWaveform: WaveformType) => {\n    setWaveform(newWaveform);\n    audioEngine.setWaveform(newWaveform);\n  };\n\n  const handleVolumeChange = (newVolume: number) => {\n    setVolume(newVolume);\n    audioEngine.setVolume(newVolume);\n  };\n\n  const handleToggleNoteNames = () => {\n    setShowNoteNames((prev) => !prev);\n  };\n\n  const handleToggleKeyLabels = () => {\n    setShowKeyLabels((prev) => !prev);\n  };\n\n  const handleToggleRecording = () => {\n    if (isRecording) {\n      // 押下中のまま録音停止されたノートを確定してコミット\n      const now = Date.now();\n      const danglingNotes: RecordedNote[] = [];\n\n      notePressStartTimesRef.current.forEach((startTime, noteId) => {\n        const duration = Math.max(now - startTime, 100);\n        const timestamp = startTime - recordStartTimeRef.current;\n        danglingNotes.push({ noteId, timestamp, duration });\n      });\n\n      notePressStartTimesRef.current.clear();\n\n      if (danglingNotes.length > 0) {\n        setRecordedNotes((prev) => [...prev, ...danglingNotes]);\n      }\n\n      setIsRecording(false);\n    } else {\n      clearAllScheduledAudio();\n      setRecordedNotes([]);\n      notePressStartTimesRef.current.clear();\n      recordStartTimeRef.current = Date.now();\n      setIsRecording(true);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePlayRecording = () => {\n    if (isPlayingRecorded) {\n      clearAllScheduledAudio();\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (recordedNotes.length === 0) return;\n\n    clearAllScheduledAudio();\n    setIsPlayingRecorded(true);\n\n    const events: PlaybackEvent[] = [];\n    let maxTime = 0;\n\n    for (const item of recordedNotes) {\n      events.push({\n        time: item.timestamp,\n        type: 'start',\n        noteId: item.noteId\n      });\n      events.push({\n        time: item.timestamp + item.duration,\n        type: 'stop',\n        noteId: item.noteId\n      });\n      if (item.timestamp + item.duration > maxTime) {\n        maxTime = item.timestamp + item.duration;\n      }\n    }\n\n    events.sort((a, b) => a.time - b.time);\n\n    sequenceEventsRef.current = events;\n    sequenceEventIndexRef.current = 0;\n    sequenceStartTimeRef.current = performance.now();\n    sequenceTotalDurationRef.current = maxTime + 300;\n    sequenceTypeRef.current = 'recorded';\n\n    if (workerRef.current) {\n      workerRef.current.postMessage('start');\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleSelectDemoSong = (index: number) => {\n    setSelectedDemoIndex(index);\n    if (isPlayingDemo) {\n      clearAllScheduledAudio();\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePlayDemoSong = () => {\n    clearAllScheduledAudio();\n    if (isRecording) {\n      setIsRecording(false);\n    }\n\n    const demo = DEMO_SONGS[selectedDemoIndex];\n    if (!demo || demo.notes.length === 0) return;\n\n    setIsPlayingDemo(true);\n\n    const events: PlaybackEvent[] = [];\n    let maxTime = 0;\n\n    for (const item of demo.notes) {\n      events.push({\n        time: item.delay,\n        type: 'start',\n        noteId: item.noteId\n      });\n      events.push({\n        time: item.delay + item.duration,\n        type: 'stop',\n        noteId: item.noteId\n      });\n      if (item.delay + item.duration > maxTime) {\n        maxTime = item.delay + item.duration;\n      }\n    }\n\n    events.sort((a, b) => a.time - b.time);\n\n    sequenceEventsRef.current = events;\n    sequenceEventIndexRef.current = 0;\n    sequenceStartTimeRef.current = performance.now();\n    sequenceTotalDurationRef.current = maxTime + 400;\n    sequenceTypeRef.current = 'demo';\n\n    if (workerRef.current) {\n      workerRef.current.postMessage('start');\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleStopDemoSong = () => {\n    clearAllScheduledAudio();\n  };\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.isComposing) return;\n      if (e.repeat || e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLSelectElement) {\n        return;\n      }\n      const key = e.key.toLowerCase();\n      const matchedKeyItem = keyToNoteMap[key];\n      if (matchedKeyItem) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        playNote(matchedKeyItem.id);\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handleKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.isComposing) return;\n      const key = e.key.toLowerCase();\n      const matchedKeyItem = keyToNoteMap[key];\n      if (matchedKeyItem) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        stopNote(matchedKeyItem.id);\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    window.addEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n      window.removeEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n      clearAllScheduledAudio();\n    };\n  }, [playNote, stopNote, clearAllScheduledAudio]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"piano-app\">\n      <header className=\"header-section\">\n        <h1 className=\"app-title\">Web ピアノスタジオ</h1>\n        <p className=\"app-subtitle\">\n          Web Audio API による本格サウンド・マウス / タッチ / PCキーボード演奏対応\n        </p>\n      </header>\n\n      <Controls\n        waveform={waveform}\n        volume={volume}\n        showNoteNames={showNoteNames}\n        showKeyLabels={showKeyLabels}\n        isRecording={isRecording}\n        isPlayingRecorded={isPlayingRecorded}\n        hasRecordedNotes={recordedNotes.length > 0}\n        recordedCount={recordedNotes.length}\n        demoSongs={DEMO_SONGS}\n        selectedDemoIndex={selectedDemoIndex}\n        isPlayingDemo={isPlayingDemo}\n        onWaveformChange={handleWaveformChange}\n        onVolumeChange={handleVolumeChange}\n        onToggleNoteNames={handleToggleNoteNames}\n        onToggleKeyLabels={handleToggleKeyLabels}\n        onToggleRecording={handleToggleRecording}\n        onPlayRecording={handlePlayRecording}\n        onSelectDemoSong={handleSelectDemoSong}\n        onPlayDemoSong={handlePlayDemoSong}\n        onStopDemoSong={handleStopDemoSong}\n      />\n\n      <PianoKeyboard\n        keys={PIANO_KEYS}\n        activeNotes={activeNotes}\n        showNoteNames={showNoteNames}\n        showKeyLabels={showKeyLabels}\n        onNoteStart={playNote}\n        onNoteStop={stopNote}\n      />\n\n      <div className=\"help-box\">\n        <strong>PCキーボード演奏方法:</strong>\n        <br />\n        白鍵: <kbd>A</kbd> (ド), <kbd>S</kbd> (レ), <kbd>D</kbd> (ミ), <kbd>F</kbd> (ファ), <kbd>G</kbd> (ソ), <kbd>H</kbd> (ラ), <kbd>J</kbd> (シ), <kbd>K</kbd> (高ド)\n        <br />\n        黒鍵: <kbd>W</kbd> (ド#), <kbd>E</kbd> (レ#), <kbd>T</kbd> (ファ#), <kbd>Y</kbd> (ソ#), <kbd>U</kbd> (ラ#)\n        <br />\n        ※ スマートフォンやタブレットでは鍵盤を直接タッチ、またはなぞってスライド演奏ができます。\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default App;","language":"tsx"}

activeVoices: Map<string, ActiveVoice> = new Map();\n  private currentWaveform: WaveformType = 'triangle';\n  private currentVolume: number = 0.5;\n\n  private initContextIfNeeded(): AudioContext {\n    if (!this.audioCtx) {\n      const AudioCtxClass = window.AudioContext || (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;\n      this.audioCtx = new AudioCtxClass();\n      \n      this.masterGain = this.audioCtx.createGain();\n      this.masterGain.gain.setValueAtTime(this.currentVolume, this.audioCtx.currentTime);\n      this.masterGain.connect(this.audioCtx.destination);\n    }\n\n    if (this.audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {\n      this.audioCtx.resume().catch((err) => {\n        console.warn('AudioContext resume was prevented or failed:', err);\n      });\n    }\n\n    return this.audioCtx;\n  }\n\n  public setWaveform(type: WaveformType): void {\n    this.currentWaveform = type;\n  }\n\n  public getWaveform(): WaveformType {\n    return this.currentWaveform;\n  }\n\n  public setVolume(volume: number): void {\n    this.currentVolume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume));\n    if (this.masterGain && this.audioCtx) {\n      const now = this.audioCtx.currentTime;\n      this.masterGain.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n      this.masterGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(this.currentVolume, now, 0.02);\n    }\n  }\n\n  public getVolume(): number {\n    return this.currentVolume;\n  }\n\n  public startNote(noteId: string, frequency: number): void {\n    const ctx = this.initContextIfNeeded();\n    if (!this.masterGain) return;\n\n    if (this.activeVoices.has(noteId)) {\n      this.stopNote(noteId);\n    }\n\n    // ボイススティーリング (最大同時発音数の制限超過時に最古のボイスを解放)\n    if (this.activeVoices.size >= osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, now);\n\n    filter.type = 'lowpass';\n    filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.min(frequency * 5, 12000), now);\n    filter.Q.setValueAtTime(1, now);\n\n    // filter.connect(voiceGain);\n    voiceGain.connect(this.masterGain);\n\n    // 再生完了時にノードの切断を安全に実施 (タイマー遅延・リーク防止)\n    osc.onended = () => {\n      osc.disconnect();\n      filter.disconnect();\n      voiceGain.disconnect();\n    };\n\n    osc.start(now);\n\n    this.activeVoices.set(noteId, {\n      oscillator: osc,\n      gainNode: voiceGain,\n      filterNode: filter\n    });\n  }\n\n  public const releaseTime = 0.18;\n\n    voice.gainNode.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n    const currentGain = Math.max(voice.gainNode.gain.value, 0.0001);\n    voice.gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(currentGain, now);\n    voice.gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now + releaseTime);\n\n    // stop() 呼び出し後に onended コールバックで disconnect() が実行される\n    voice.oscillator.stop(now + releaseTime + 0.02);\n\n    this.activeVoices.delete(noteId);\n  }\n\n  public stopAllNotes(): void {\n    const noteIds = Array.from(this.activeVoices.keys());\n    for
